<script type="text/javascript">
		require(['jquery', 'domReady'], function($, domReady) {

	domReady(function() {

       /* ************************************************************************************************************************

	START HERO BANNER

        ************************************************************************************************************************ */

/*
    var _heroBannerPicture = $(".hero-banner .shapes");
    var _heroBannerShape =  $(".hero-banner .image");
    $( window ).scroll(function(e) {
    	var scrollTopPicture = $(window).scrollTop() / 10;
    	var scrollTopShape = $(window).scrollTop() / 5;
 		_heroBannerPicture.css("transform","translateY(" + scrollTopPicture + "px)");	
 		_heroBannerShape.css("transform","translateY(-" + scrollTopShape + "px)");	
    });
*/

    /* ************************************************************************************************************************

	END  HERO BANNER

        ************************************************************************************************************************ */


	/* SCROLL ANIMATE */

	 function scrollAnimate() {
		var windowScrollTop 	= $(window).scrollTop();
		var windowHeight 		= $(window).height();
		var animations = 	[
								{
									selector : ".columns-left",
									class: "columns-scroll-animate"
								},
								{
									selector : ".columns-right",
									class: "columns-scroll-animate"
								},
								{
									selector : ".columns-full",
									class: "columns-scroll-animate"
								}
							]

		$(animations).each(function(iAnimation){
			var elements = $(animations[iAnimation].selector);
			$(elements).each(function(iElement){
				if (windowScrollTop + windowHeight > $(elements[iElement]).offset().top){
					$(elements[iElement]).addClass(animations[iAnimation].class);
				}
			});
		});
	}
	
	$(window).on('resize', scrollAnimate);
	$(window).on('scroll', scrollAnimate);
	
    if ( navigator.userAgent.match(/Android/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/webOS/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/iPhone/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/iPad/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/iPod/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/BlackBerry/i)
	 || navigator.userAgent.match(/Windows Phone/i)){
		setTimeout(function(){
			scrollAnimate();
		},500);}
	else{
          
		scrollAnimate();
           
	}
	
});
    
   
});</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
		require(['jquery', 'domReady', 'https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/PapaParse/4.3.7/papaparse.min.js'], function($, domReady, Papa) {
  domReady(function() {
    
    var highlightsBar = {
      init: function() {
        this.parseCSV();
      },

      parseCSV: function() {
        var config = {
          delimiter: ",",  // auto-detect
          newline: "",  // auto-detect
          quoteChar: '"',
          header: true,
          dynamicTyping: false,
          preview: 0,
          encoding: "",
          worker: false,
          comments: false,
          step: undefined,
          complete: undefined,
          error: undefined,
          download: false,
          skipEmptyLines: true,
          chunk: undefined,
          fastMode: undefined,
          beforeFirstChunk: undefined,
          withCredentials: undefined
        };

        if (MOBILE_MENU && MOBILE_MENU.length) {
          Papa.parse(MOBILE_MENU, $.extend({}, config, { complete: function(json) { highlightsBar.onParseComplete('mobile', json); } }));
        }


        if (BUTTON_ICONS && BUTTON_ICONS.length) {
          Papa.parse(BUTTON_ICONS, $.extend({}, config, { complete: function(json) { highlightsBar.onParseComplete('icons', json); } }));
        }
        if (DESKTOP_BAR && DESKTOP_BAR.length) {
          Papa.parse(DESKTOP_BAR, $.extend({}, config, { complete: function(json) { highlightsBar.onParseComplete('desktop', json); } }));
        }

      },

      onParseComplete: function(target, json) {
        switch(target) {
            case 'mobile':
                var menu = this.formatMenu(json);
                this.createMenuMobile(menu);
                this.initAccordion();
                break;

            case 'icons':
                this.createIconButtons(json);
                break;

            case 'desktop':
                this.createMenuDesktop(json);
                break;
        }
      },

      formatMenu: function(json) {
        var subMenu = {};
        var previousIndex = 0;

        var menu = $.map(json.data, function(val, i) {
          if (val.MENU_LABEL && val.SUBMENU_LABEL) {
            previousIndex = i;
          }

          if (val.SUBMENU_LABEL) {
            subMenu[previousIndex] = subMenu[previousIndex] || [];

            subMenu[previousIndex].push({
              SUBMENU_LABEL: val.SUBMENU_LABEL,
              SUBMENU_LINK: val.SUBMENU_LINK,
              SUBMENU_TRACKING: val.SUBMENU_TRACKING
            });
          }

          if (val.MENU_LABEL) {
            var item = {
              MENU_LABEL: val.MENU_LABEL,
              MENU_LINK: val.MENU_LINK,
              MENU_TRACKING: val.MENU_TRACKING
            };

            return item;
          }
        });

        $.each(menu, function(i) {
          if (subMenu[i] && subMenu[i].length) {
            menu[i].SUBMENU = subMenu[i];
          }
        });

        return menu;
      },

      createMenuMobile: function(json) {
        var menu = '<ul class="menu">';

        $.each(json, function(index, firstLevel) {
          var hasSubmenu = firstLevel.SUBMENU && firstLevel.SUBMENU.length;
          var subMenuClass = hasSubmenu ? 'with-submenu' : '';

          var li = '<li class="' + subMenuClass + '"><a href="' + firstLevel.MENU_LINK + firstLevel.MENU_TRACKING + '">' + firstLevel.MENU_LABEL + '</a>';

          if (hasSubmenu) {
            var submenu = '<ul class="submenu">';

            $.each(firstLevel.SUBMENU, function(index, secondLevel) {
              submenu += '<li><a href="' + secondLevel.SUBMENU_LINK + secondLevel.SUBMENU_TRACKING + '">' + secondLevel.SUBMENU_LABEL + '</a></li>';
            });

            submenu += '</ul>';
            li += submenu;
          }

          li += '</li>';
          menu += li;
        });

        menu += '</ul>';
        $('#highlights-mobile').append(menu);
      },

      initAccordion: function() {
        $(document).on('click', '#highlights-mobile ul.menu li a', function(e){
          if ($(this).next().hasClass('submenu')) {
            e.preventDefault();
            $(this).next().slideToggle(200);
            $(this).parent('li').toggleClass('open');
          }
        });
      },

      createIconButtons: function(json) {
        var row = '<ul class="button-icons row">';

        $.each(json.data, function(index, icon) {
          row += '<li class="columns small-4 end"><a style="background-image: url(' + icon.IMG + ')" href="' + icon.LINK + icon.TRACKING + '"><span>' + icon.LABEL + '</span></a></li>';
        });

        row += '</ul>';
        $('#highlights-mobile').append(row);
      },

      createMenuDesktop: function(json) {
        $.each(json.data, function(index, item) {
          var firstOrLast = '';
          switch(index) {
              case 0:
                  firstOrLast = 'first-element';
                  break;

              case json.data.length - 1:
                  firstOrLast = 'last-element';
                  break;
          }
          $('#highlights-desktop .mobile-slider').append('<span class="link-element ' + firstOrLast + '"><a href="' + item.MENU_LINK + item.MENU_TRACKING + '">' +'<span class="item-title">'+ item.MENU_LABEL+'</span>'+ '</a> </span>');


        });
      }
    };

    highlightsBar.init();

  });
});</script>

<div class="customhtml2 section"><script type="text/javascript">
		document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function(){ 
	//setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2");}, 2000);
	//setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons p");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons p");}, 2000);

     //setHeight(".row5 h2");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row5 h2");}, 2000);
       //setHeight(".row5 p");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row5 p");}, 2000);
}, false);

function setHeight(target){
	var maxHeight = 0;
	document.querySelectorAll(target).forEach(item=>{                      
		item.style.height="auto";
	});                
	if(window.innerWidth>=640){
		document.querySelectorAll(target).forEach(item=>{                      
			if(maxHeight<item.offsetHeight){
				maxHeight=item.offsetHeight;
			}
		});
		document.querySelectorAll(target).forEach(item=>{                      
			item.style.height=maxHeight+"px";
		});
	}                            
}


var element = document.querySelectorAll(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2")[0]
element.addEventListener("load", function(){
	//setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2");}, 2000);
	//setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons p");
	setTimeout(()=>{setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons p");}, 2000);

});



window.onresize = function(event) {
	setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons h2");
	setHeight(".row3-wrapper .title-text-buttons p");

     
   setHeight(".row5 h2");
   setHeight(".row5 p");
}</script>
</div>

/* -

– Partial Carousel Styles –
How to display partial carousel content for unfilled widgets (like a call-to-action banner).

The following steps assume we are using a 3 image carousel:

1) Show the ".olapic-carousel-partial-container" div when there are a certain number of photos available.
In this case, the partial-container will show only when there are 0, 1, or 2 photos in the carousel.
(olapic_items_n is a utility class, which is appended to the carousel when 'n' number of photos are
loaded into it):
.olapic_items_0 .olapic-carousel-partial-container,
.olapic_items_1 .olapic-carousel-partial-container,
.olapic_items_2 .olapic-carousel-partial-container
{ display: block }

2) set the ".olapic-carousel-list-container" div width to 100% for the 0, 1, 2 images
.olapic_items_0 .olapic-carousel-list-container,
.olapic_items_1 .olapic-carousel-list-container,
.olapic_items_2 .olapic-carousel-list-container
{ width: 100%; }

3) Go to @media (max-width: 480px) in your responsive styles,
and optionally hide the carousel images and only show the ".olapic-carousel-partial-container". Responsive styles section is at the bottom of this stylesheet

- */

/* - End of Partial carousel container - */

Cette semaine j’ai eu la chance, grâce à twix_uap fondateur du média urbanartparis, d’assister à la deuxième édition de leur évent labelvalettefestival 🙏🏼 aux côtés de arthur_vlog_streetart, conteur de street dream.

ardpg est celui qui a investi La Chapelle et réinventé la foi du graffiti « au nom du Père du fils et du graff » et le « # » qui surplombe le tout « ON ADORE ! ». <script>

	require(["jquery"],function($) {
        $( document ).ready( function() {
			var el = $('.cookie-policy-container');
			
			if (document.cookie.indexOf('TBLCookiesAccepted=true') > -1) {
				el.parent().remove();
			} else {
				el.removeClass('hide');
			}
			$('.cookie-policy .close').on('click', function () {
				write_cookie ('TBLCookiesAccepted', true, '/');
				//document.cookie = 'TBLCookiesAccepted=true';
				el.parent().remove();
			});

			function write_cookie (name, value, path){
				// Build the expiration date string:
				var expiration_date = new Date();
				expiration_date.setFullYear(expiration_date.getFullYear() + 1);
		
				// Build the set-cookie string:
				var cookie_string = escape(name) + "=" + escape(value) +
						"; expires=" + expiration_date.toUTCString();
				if (path != null)
						cookie_string += "; path=" + path;
		
				// Create/update the cookie:
				document.cookie = cookie_string;
			}

		});
	});
	
	
</script></div>

<script>
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // LOAD COMPONENT

    window._load_component_caches = {};
    function LOAD_COMPONENT(_componentPath, _componentName, _extensions,  _callBack){
        if (window._load_component_caches[_componentName] && window._load_component_caches[_componentName].cache == 'finished'){
            // Component has been alredy loaded
            if (_callBack){
                _callBack(window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._json);
                return;
            }
        }

        if (window._load_component_caches[_componentName] && window._load_component_caches[_componentName].cache == 'inProgress'){
            // Waiting for caching process
            var _waitingForCachingProcess = setInterval(function(){
                if (window._load_component_caches[_componentName].cache == 'finished'){
                    clearInterval(_waitingForCachingProcess);
                    if (this._callBack){
                        if (window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._json)
                            this._callBack(window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._json);
                        else
                            this._callBack(undefined);
                    }
                }
            }.bind({_callBack:_callBack}),250);
            return;
        }
            
        var _json = undefined;
        var _done = 0;
        window._load_component_caches[_componentName] = {'cache': 'inProgress'};
        var _i = 0;
        for(_i = 0;  _i <= _extensions.length-1 ; _i++)
        {

            var xhttp = new XMLHttpRequest();
            xhttp._extension = _extensions[_i];
            xhttp.onreadystatechange = function()
            {
                
                if (this.readyState == 4 && this.status == 200)
                {
                    switch(this._extension)
                    {
                        case "json":
                            response = JSON.parse(this.responseText);
                            if (_json === undefined)
                                _json = {};
                            _json[Object.keys(response)[0]] = response[Object.keys(response)[0]];
                            window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._json = _json;
                        break;
                        case "css":
                            var _head = document.createElement('head');
                            _head.innerHTML = this.response;
                            var _css = _head.firstChild;
                            document.querySelector("head").appendChild(_css);
                            window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._css = this.response;
                        break;
                        case "js":
                            var _head = document.createElement('head');
                            _head.innerHTML = this.response;
                            _js = _head.querySelectorAll('script');
                            var _css = _head.querySelectorAll('style');


                            if (_css)
                            {
                                for (var _i = 0 ; _i <= _css.length-1 ; _i++)
                                {
                                    document.querySelector("head").appendChild(_css[_i]);
                                }
                            }

                             if (_js)
                            {
                                for (var _i = 0 ; _i <= _js.length-1 ; _i++)
                                {
                                    eval(_js[_i].text);
                                }
                            }

                            
                            window._load_component_caches[_componentName]._js = this.response;   
                        break;
                        
                    }

                     _done++;

                    if (_done == _extensions.length)
                    {
                        window._load_component_caches[_componentName].cache = 'finished';
                        setTimeout(function(){
                            if (_callBack)
                            {
                                _callBack(_json);
                            }
                        },250);
                    }
                }
            }

            xhttp.open("GET", _componentPath + _componentName + "/" + _componentName + "-" + _extensions[_i] + ".html", true);
            xhttp.send();

            
        }
    };
    
    // LOAD COMPONENT
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
    // ********************************************************************************
</script>

    LOAD_COMPONENT("/core-components/","core-footer", ["js"],  function(){});
</script>

<script>
require(["jquery"], function($) {
    $(document).ready(function() {

        function write_permanent_cookie(name, value, path) {
            // Build the expiration date string:
            var expiration_date = new Date();
            expiration_date.setFullYear(expiration_date.getFullYear() + 1);
            // Build the set-cookie string:
            var cookie_string = escape(name) + "=" + escape(value) +
                "; expires=" + expiration_date.toUTCString();
            if (path != null)
                cookie_string += "; path=" + path;
            // Create/update the cookie:
            document.cookie = cookie_string;
        }

        function write_session_cookie(name, value, path) {
            // not executed if the close button is clicked from the thank you page
            if (document.cookie.indexOf('TBLCookiesFFS18=true') == -1) {
                var cookie_string = escape(name) + "=" + escape(value)
                if (path != null)
                    cookie_string += "; path=" + path;
                    document.cookie = cookie_string;
            }
        }

        var getUrlParameter = function getUrlParameter(sParam) {
            var sPageURL = decodeURIComponent(window.location.search.substring(1)),
                sURLVariables = sPageURL.split('&'),
                sParameterName, i;

            for (i = 0; i < sURLVariables.length; i++) {
                sParameterName = sURLVariables[i].split('=');

                if (sParameterName[0].toLowerCase() === sParam) {
                    return sParameterName[1] === undefined ? true : sParameterName[1];
                }
            }
        };
        // force selectTab to true to remove parameter presence check

            var prefix_es_es_AEM = "";
            var prefix_fr_fr_AEM = "";
            var prefix_it_it_AEM = "";
            var prefix_nl_nl_AEM = "";
            var prefix_de_at_AEM = "/de_at";
            var prefix_de_be_AEM = "/de_be";
            var prefix_de_ch_AEM = "/de_ch";
            var prefix_de_lu_AEM = "/de_lu";
            var prefix_en_ch_AEM = "/en_ch";
            var prefix_en_dk_AEM = "/en_dk";
            var prefix_en_ie_AEM = "/en_ie";
            var prefix_en_no_AEM = "/en_no";
            var prefix_en_pt_AEM = "/en_pt";
            var prefix_en_se_AEM = "/en_se";
            var prefix_fr_be_AEM = "/fr_be";
            var prefix_fr_ch_AEM = "/fr_ch";
            var prefix_fr_lu_AEM = "/fr_lu";
            var prefix_it_ch_AEM = "/it_ch";
            var prefix_nl_be_AEM = "/nl_be";
            var prefix_nl_lu_AEM = "/nl_lu";
            var lang = "";
            var url = "";

            /*********** END VARIABLES INITIALIZATION ************/

            /*********** CUSTOMIZABLE AREA ************/
            var url1_DE = "/kundenservice/geschaftsbedingungen-fur-aktion.html";
            var url1_EN = "/customer-service/terms-and-conditions-promo.html";
            var url1_ES = "/atencion-al-cliente/terminos-y-condiciones-de-la-promocion.html";
            var url1_FR = "/aide-et-contact/conditions-generales-de-la-promotion.html";
            var url1_IT = "/servizio-clienti/termini-e-condizioni-della-promozione.html";
            var url1_NL = "/klantenservice/algemene-voorwaarden-van-de-promotie.html";

            var selector1 = ".link-1";

            var mapURL1 = {
                "de_at": prefix_de_at_AEM + url1_DE,
                "de_be": prefix_de_be_AEM + url1_DE,
                "de_ch": prefix_de_ch_AEM + url1_DE,
                "de_de": prefix_de_de_AEM + url1_DE,
                "de_lu": prefix_de_lu_AEM + url1_DE,
                "en_ch": prefix_en_ch_AEM + url1_EN,
                "en_dk": prefix_en_dk_AEM + url1_EN,
                "en_ie": prefix_en_ie_AEM + url1_EN,

                "es_es": prefix_es_es_AEM + url1_ES,

                "fr_ch": prefix_fr_ch_AEM + url1_FR,

                "fr_fr": prefix_fr_fr_AEM + url1_FR,
                "it_ch": prefix_it_ch_AEM + url1_IT,
                "it_it": prefix_it_it_AEM + url1_IT,
                "nl_be": prefix_nl_be_AEM + url1_NL,
                "nl_lu": prefix_nl_lu_AEM + url1_NL,
                "nl_nl": prefix_nl_nl_AEM + url1_NL
            };

            var selectorArray = [selector1];
            var mapURLArray = [mapURL1];
            /*********** END - CUSTOMIZABLE AREA ************/

            /****** LOGIC TO RETRIEVE CORRECT UR BASED ON COUNTRY/LANGUAGE COMBINATION ******/

            //var href =  $('.country-name.region').attr('href');

            var hostname = $(location).attr('hostname');
            var pathname = $(location).attr('pathname');
            var domain = hostname.substr(hostname.length - 3);

            var mapLangs = {
                ".de": "de_de",
                ".uk": "en_gb",
                ".es": "es_es",
                ".fr": "fr_fr",
                ".it": "it_it",
                ".nl": "nl_nl"
            };

            var mapLangsDefault = {
                ".be": "fr_be",
                ".lu": "fr_lu",
                ".ch": "de_ch",
                ".at": "de_at",
                ".dk": "en_dk",
                ".ie": "en_ie",
                ".no": "en_no",
                ".pt": "en_pt",
                ".se": "en_se"
            };

            var mapMultiLangs = {
                "/de/tbl-ch/": "de_ch",
                "/fr/tbl-ch/": "fr_ch",
                "/it/tbl-ch/": "it_ch",
                "/en/tbl-ch/": "en_ch",
                "/fr/tbl-be/": "fr_be",
                "/nl/tbl-be/": "nl_be",
                "/de/tbl-be/": "de_be",
                "/fr/tbl-lu/": "fr_lu",
                "/nl/tbl-lu/": "nl_lu",
                "/de/tbl-lu/": "de_lu",
                "/de/tbl-at/": "de_at",
                "/en/tbl-dk/": "en_dk",
                "/en/tbl-ie/": "en_ie",
                "/en/tbl-no/": "en_no",
                "/en/tbl-pt/": "en_pt",
                "/en/tbl-se/": "en_se"
            };

            var i = 0;

            if ($.inArray(domain, firstLevelDomainMonoLang) > -1) {

                lang = getLang(domain);

            } else if ($.inArray(domain, firstLevelDomainMultiLang) > -1) {

                // check if the page is a PLP or PDP
                if (pathname.indexOf("/shop/") > -1) {
                    WCSLang = pathname.substr(5, 11);
                    lang = getMultiLangs(WCSLang);
                } else {
                    // content page
                    lang = pathname.substr(1, 5);
                    // content page without default language_country specified
                    if (lang.indexOf("_") < 0) {
                        lang = getLangsDefault(domain);
                    }
                }

            }

            // SET CORRECT CONTENT
            setContent(lang);
            initializeComponentBannerSlideDown(lang);

            // cycle to assign the correct url to the correspondant selector
            while (mapURLArray[i]) {
                url = getURL(lang, mapURLArray[i]);
                $(selectorArray[i]).attr("href", url);
                i++;
            }

            function getLang(k) {
                return mapLangs[k];
            }

            function getURL(k, mapURL) {
                return mapURL[k];
            }

            function getMultiLangs(k) {
                return mapMultiLangs[k];
            }

            function getLangsDefault(k) {
                return mapLangsDefault[k];
            }

            /****** END - LOGIC TO RETRIEVE CORRECT UR BASED ON COUNTRY/LANGUAGE COMBINATION ******/
            // LINK HANDLER END
        }

        /* ******************************************************

        END POPULATE LINK

        ****************************************************** */ 

        populateLinks();
       

        });
    });

          
    
    
    
</script>
<script>
            /* ******************************************************

            START BANNER-SLIDE-DOWN COMPONENT STANDARD JS 

            ****************************************************** */   

            function ComponentBannerSlideDown(){
                var animation               = false;
                var component               = undefined;
                var componentContent        = undefined;
                var componentCloseBtn       = undefined;
                var target                  = undefined;
                var closeCallBack           = undefined;
                var openCallBack            = undefined;
                var preOpenCallBack         = undefined;
                var preCloseCallBack        = undefined;

                this.toggle = function(){
                    if (component){
                        if (component.classList.contains("show")){
                            this.hide();
                        }
                        else{
                            this.show();}
                    }
                };

                this.appendComponent = function(_target, _componentName, _content, _closeCallBack, _openCallBack, _preOpenCallBack, _preCloseCallBack){
                    closeCallBack       = _closeCallBack;
                    openCallBack        = _openCallBack;
                    preOpenCallBack     = _preOpenCallBack
                    preCloseCallBack    = _preCloseCallBack
                    target              = $(_target)[0];
                    target.innerHTML    = target.innerHTML + "<div class='ComponentBannerSlideDown' id='" + _componentName + "'><div class='ComponentBannerSlideDownContent'><div class='close'><div class='closeLine1'></div><div class='closeLine2'></div></div>" + _content + "</div></div>";
                    component           = document.getElementById(_componentName);
                    componentContent    = component.children[0];
                    componentCloseBtn   = document.querySelector("#" +  _componentName + " .close");
                    componentCloseBtn.addEventListener("click",this.toggle.bind(this));
                    
                };
              
                this.show = function(){
                    if (!animation){
                        animation = true;
                        if (preOpenCallBack){
                            preOpenCallBack();
                        }
                        component.style.height      = componentContent.clientHeight + "px";
                        component.style.visibility  = "visible";
                        component.classList.add("show");
                        setTimeout(function(){
                            component.style.height = "auto"
                         },500);

                        var cnt = 0;
                        for(i=0; i < componentContent.children.length ; i++){
                             if (!componentContent.children[i].classList.contains('noInitialShow')){
                                cnt++;
                                setTimeout(function(i){
                                    componentContent.children[i].classList.add("show");
                                },cnt*100,i);
                            }
                        }
                        setTimeout(function(){
                          animation=false;
                          if (openCallBack){
                            openCallBack();
                          }
                        },500);
                    }
                };

                this.hide = function(){
                    if (!animation){
                        component.style.height         = componentContent.clientHeight + "px";
                        animation = true;
                        if (preCloseCallBack){
                            preCloseCallBack();
                        }  
                        var cnt = 0;
                        for(i=0; i < componentContent.children.length ; i++){
                            cnt++;
                            setTimeout(function(i){
                                componentContent.children[(componentContent.children.length-1) - i].classList.remove("show");
                            },i*100,i);
                        }
                        setTimeout(function(){
                            component.classList.remove("show");
                            component.style.height  = "0px";
                            setTimeout(function(){
                                component.style.visibility  = "hidden";
                                animation=false;
                                if (closeCallBack){
                                        closeCallBack();
                                }
                        },500);
                    },cnt*100);      
                };
            };
        };

         /* ******************************************************

         END BANNER-SLIDE-DOWN COMPONENT STANDARD JS 

         ****************************************************** */  

</script>

            if(myComponentBannerSlideDown.toggle){
                myComponentBannerSlideDown.toggle();
               }
        }

        function navBarSlideDownArrowOpen(){
             document.getElementById("navBarSlideDownArrow").style.transform = "rotate(-90deg)";
        }

          function navBarSlideDownArrowClose(){
             document.getElementById("navBarSlideDownArrow").style.transform = "rotate(90deg)";
        }

        function writeCoockie()
        {
              var cookie_string = escape("TBLCookiesGenesis2") + "=" + escape(true);
              var expirationDate = new Date();
              expirationDate = new Date(expirationDate.getTime() + ((60*(60*1000))*3)      );
              cookie_string += ";expires=" + expirationDate.toGMTString() + "; path=" + '/';
              document.cookie = cookie_string;
        }
